{\footnotesize\ttfamily unsigned \mbox{\hyperlink{dcache_8h_ad65b480f8c8270356b45a9890f6499ae}{int}} trace\+\_\+call\+\_\+bpf (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{struct \mbox{\hyperlink{structtrace__event__call}{trace\+\_\+event\+\_\+call}} $\ast$}]{call,  }\item[{void $\ast$}]{ctx }\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

trace\+\_\+call\+\_\+bpf -\/ invoke B\+PF program @call\+: tracepoint event @ctx\+: opaque context pointer

kprobe handlers execute B\+PF programs via this helper. Can be used from static tracepoints in the future.

Return\+: B\+PF programs always return an integer which is interpreted by kprobe handler as\+: 0 -\/ return from kprobe (event is filtered out) 1 -\/ store kprobe event into ring buffer Other values are reserved and currently alias to 1 Here is the call graph for this function\+:
